lines.append("**Both D1 and D2 are FALSIFIED.**\\n\\n")\n        lines.append("Mean ΔR²_color < 0.30 OR lower 95% CI < 0.18 on all sufficiently-powered arms.\\n")\n        lines.append("This implies # ARM definitions

ARMS = [
    {
        "name": "D1 (Contrastive, batch-level)",
        "primary_objective": "contrastive",
        "contrastive_weight": 5.0,
        "temperature": 0.1,
        "sfa_weight": 0.0,
        "mask_dyn_sim": True,
        "coord_vicreg": True,
        "var_weight": 25.0,
        "cov_weight": 25.0,
        "sim_weight": 1.0,
        "lr": 3e-4,
        "batch_size": 32,
        "d_max": 8,
        "d_t": 3,
        "pos_encoding": "none",
        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,
        "ramp_sfa": False,
    },
    {
        "name": "D2 (SFA, variance-ramped)",
        "primary_objective": "sfa",
        "sfa_weight": 5.0,          # target value
        "mask_dyn_sim": True,
        "coord_vicreg": True,
        "var_weight": 25.0,
        "cov_weight": 25.0,
        "sim_weight": 1.0,
        "lr": 3e-4,
        "batch_size": 32,
        "d_max": 8,
        "d_t": 3,
        "pos_encoding": "none",
        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,
        "ramp_sfa": True,
        "ramp_steps": 4000,
        "ramp_max": 5.0,
    },
]

SEEDS_ORIGINAL = [7, 17, 31, 53, 71, 83, 97, 113, 127, 149]
SEEDS_FRESH  = [101, 103, 107, 109, 131, 137, 139, 151, 157, 163]
SEEDS_NEW    = [173, 179, 181, 191, 193, 197, 199, 211, 223, 227]
SEEDS = SEEDS_ORIGINAL + SEEDS_FRESH + SEEDS_NEW  # 30 seeds
HARD_SEEDS = [53, 71]


def _sanitize_arm_name(name):
    return name.lower().replace(' ', '_').replace('(', '').replace(')', '').replace('+', '').replace('=', '_').replace("'", "")


def _flatten_result(res, runs_dir):
    flat = {k: v for k, v in res.items() if k not in ("centroid_mse_per_object", "r2_per_dim", "dim_to_obj")}
    flat["per_dim_std"] = str(res.get("per_dim_std", []))
    flat["per_dim_std_train"] = str(res.get("per_dim_std_train", []))
    safe_name = _sanitize_arm_name(res["arm"])
    run_id = f"{safe_name}_seed{res['seed']}"
    csv_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{run_id}.csv")
    json_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{run_id}.json")
    pd.DataFrame([flat]).to_csv(csv_path, index=False)
    with open(json_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(res, f, indent=2, default=str)
    return csv_path


def _run_single_worker(args_tuple):
    arm, seed, device_str, dry_run, runs_dir, checkpoints_dir = args_tuple
    device = torch.device(device_str); torch.set_num_threads(1)
    name = arm["name"]
    print(f"[{name}] seed={seed} -> starting on {device} (dry_run={dry_run})")
    eval_res, model, logs = run_single(arm, seed, device, dry_run=dry_run)
    csv_path = _flatten_result(eval_res, runs_dir)
    safe_name = _sanitize_arm_name(eval_res["arm"])
    ckpt_path = os.path.join(checkpoints_dir, f"{safe_name}_seed{seed}.pt")
    torch.save(model.state_dict(), ckpt_path)
    logs_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{safe_name}_seed{seed}_logs.csv")
    pd.DataFrame(logs).to_csv(logs_path, index=False)
    return eval_res


def _generate_analysis(df_all, results_dir):
    lines = []
    lines.append("# Iter_030 ARM 2 — M2 Decisive Test Analysis\n\n")
    lines.append("This analysis tests whether D1 (batch-level temporal contrastive NT-Xent) or "
                 "D2 (variance-ramped SFA) achieves mean ΔR²_color ≥ 0.30 with variance-stability "
                 "(lower 95% CI ≥ 0.18) on a 30-seed union bank.\n\n")
    lines.append("**Dual collapse criterion:** collapsed = collapsed_eval OR collapsed_train\n")
    lines.append("**Contrastive implementation:** Existing batch-level NT-Xent in model.forward "
                 "(same batch index as positive pair, cross-batch as negatives). "
                 "Object-level temporal matching NOT implemented — uses weaker batch-level signal.\n")
    lines.append("**D2 ramp:** sfa_weight linearly 0 → 5.0 over steps 1–4000, then held at 5.0 for steps 4001–8000.\n\n")
    lines.append("---\n\n")
    arm_names = [a["name"] for a in ARMS]

    # Per-arm summary
    lines.append("## Per-Arm Summary (all 30 seeds)\n\n")
    for arm_name in arm_names:
        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]
        if len(df_arm) == 0:
            continue
        lines.append(f"### {arm_name}\n")
        lines.append(f"- N seeds: {len(df_arm)}\n")
        cr_dual = df_arm["collapsed"].mean(); cc_dual = int(df_arm["collapsed"].sum())
        cr_eval = df_arm["collapsed_eval"].mean(); cc_eval = int(df_arm["collapsed_eval"].sum())
        cr_train = df_arm["collapsed_train"].mean(); cc_train = int(df_arm["collapsed_train"].sum())
        lines.append(f"- Collapse rate (dual): {cr_dual:.2f} ({cc_dual}/{len(df_arm)})\n")
        lines.append(f"- Collapse rate (eval-only): {cr_eval:.2f} ({cc_eval}/{len(df_arm)})\n")
        lines.append(f"- Collapse rate (train-only): {cr_train:.2f} ({cc_train}/{len(df_arm)})\n")
        dq_count = int(df_arm["disqualified"].sum()) if "disqualified" in df_arm.columns else 0
        if dq_count > 0:
            lines.append(f"- **Disqualified seeds (loss > 50):** {dq_count}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Mean ΔR²_color (all): {df_arm['delta_r2_color'].mean():.4f} ± {df_arm['delta_r2_color'].std():.4f}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Mean centroid MSE: {df_arm['centroid_mse_mean'].mean():.2f}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Mean abs corr: {df_arm['vicreg_mean_abs_corr'].mean():.3f}\n")
        if "param_count" in df_arm.columns:
            lines.append(f"- Parameter count: {int(df_arm['param_count'].iloc[0])}\n")
        lines.append("\n")

    # Non-collapsed summary + 95% CI
    lines.append("## Non-Collapsed Seed Analysis (preregistered gate)\n\n")
    lines.append("Falsification criterion per arm:\n")
    lines.append("- **FALSIFIED** if mean ΔR²_color < 0.30 OR lower 95% CI < 0.18 on non-collapsed seeds.\n")
    lines.append("- If n_non-collapsed < 5, flagged as **underpowered** (cannot compute stable CI).\n\n")

    for arm_name in arm_names:
        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]
        df_nc = df_arm[~df_arm["collapsed"]] if len(df_arm) > 0 else df_arm
        n_nc = len(df_nc)
        lines.append(f"### {arm_name} — non-collapsed\n")
        lines.append(f"- N non-collapsed: {n_nc} / {len(df_arm)}\n")
        if n_nc == 0:
            lines.append("- **ALL seeds collapsed — ARM FALSIFIED (no decodable representations).**\n\n")
            continue
        mean_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].mean()
        std_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].std(ddof=1)
        se_dr2 = std_dr2 / math.sqrt(n_nc) if n_nc > 0 else float('nan')
        ci_lower = mean_dr2 - 1.96 * se_dr2 if n_nc > 0 else float('nan')
        lines.append(f"- Mean ΔR²_color: {mean_dr2:.4f} ± {std_dr2:.4f}\n")
        lines.append(f"- SE: {se_dr2:.4f}\n")
        if n_nc < 5:
            lines.append(f"- 95% CI lower bound: **underpowered** (n={n_nc} < 5)\n")
            lines.append(f"- Verdict: **INDETERMINATE** — insufficient non-collapsed seeds to compute CI.\n\n")
            continue
        lines.append(f"- 95% CI lower bound: {ci_lower:.4f}\n")
        if mean_dr2 >= 0.30 and ci_lower >= 0.18:
            lines.append("- **VERDICT: NOT FALSIFIED** — mean ≥ 0.30 and lower CI ≥ 0.18.\n\n")
        elif mean_dr2 < 0.30 and ci_lower < 0.18:
            lines.append("- **VERDICT: FALSIFIED** — mean < 0.30 AND lower CI < 0.18.\n\n")
        elif mean_dr2 < 0.30:
            lines.append("- **VERDICT: FALSIFIED** — mean < 0.30.\n\n")
        else:
            lines.append("- **VERDICT: FALSIFIED** — lower CI < 0.18 (variance-stability gate failed).\n\n")

    # Hard-seed table
    lines.append("## Hard-Seed Table (Seeds 53, 71)\n\n")
    lines.append("| seed | arm | collapsed | ΔR²_color | centroid_mse | mean_abs_corr | per_dim_std |\n")
    lines.append("|------|-----|-----------|-----------|--------------|---------------|-------------|\n")
    for seed_val in HARD_SEEDS:
        for arm_name in arm_names:
            df_match = df_all[(df_all["seed"] == seed_val) & (df_all["arm"] == arm_name)]
            if len(df_match) == 0:
                continue
            row = df_match.iloc[0]
            c = "Y" if row["collapsed"] else "N"
            dr2 = f"{row.get('delta_r2_color', 0.0):.4f}"
            cmse = f"{row.get('centroid_mse_mean', 0.0):.2f}"
            mac = f"{row.get('vicreg_mean_abs_corr', 0.0):.3f}"
            pds = str(row.get("per_dim_std", "[]"))
            lines.append(f"| {seed_val} | {arm_name} | {c} | {dr2} | {cmse} | {mac} | {pds} |\n")
    lines.append("\n")

    # Seed-bank breakdown
    lines.append("## Seed-Bank Breakdown\n\n")
    lines.append("| arm | bank | n | mean_ΔR²_color | collapse_rate | mean_centroid_mse |\n")
    lines.append("|-----|------|---|----------------|---------------|-------------------|\n")
    bank_map = {
        "original": SEEDS_ORIGINAL,
        "fresh": SEEDS_FRESH,
        "new": SEEDS_NEW,
    }
    for arm_name in arm_names:
        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]
        if len(df_arm) == 0:
            continue
        for bank_name, bank_seeds in bank_map.items():
            df_bank = df_arm[df_arm["seed"].isin(bank_seeds)]
            if len(df_bank) == 0:
                continue
            dr2 = f"{df_bank['delta_r2_color'].mean():.4f}"
            cr = f"{df_bank['collapsed'].mean():.2f}"
            cmse = f"{df_bank['centroid_mse_mean'].mean():.2f}"
            lines.append(f"| {arm_name} | {bank_name} ({len(df_bank)}) | {len(df_bank)} | {dr2} | {cr} | {cmse} |\n")
    lines.append("\n")

    # Pre-registered outcome
    lines.append("## Pre-Registered Outcome Classification\n\n")
    lines.append("**Hypothesis:** Temporal identity contrastive binding (D1) or variance-ramped SFA (D2) "
                 "achieves mean ΔR²_color ≥ 0.30 on 30-seed union with lower 95% CI ≥ 0.18.\n\n")

    both_falsified = True
    for arm_name in arm_names:
        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]
        df_nc = df_arm[~df_arm["collapsed"]] if len(df_arm) > 0 else df_arm
        n_nc = len(df_nc)
        if n_nc >= 5:
            mean_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].mean()
            std_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].std(ddof=1)
            se_dr2 = std_dr2 / math.sqrt(n_nc)
            ci_lower = mean_dr2 - 1.96 * se_dr2
            if mean_dr2 >= 0.30 and ci_lower >= 0.18:
                both_falsified = False
                lines.append(f"**{arm_name}: NOT FALSIFIED** — mean ΔR²_color = {mean_dr2:.4f}, "
                             f"lower CI = {ci_lower:.4f} ≥ 0.18.\n\n")
        elif n_nc > 0:
            # Underpowered but check mean anyway
            mean_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].mean()
            if mean_dr2 >= 0.30:
                both_falsified = False

    if both_falsified:
        lines.append("**Both D1 and D2 are FALSIFIED.**\n\n")
        lines.append("Mean ΔR²_color < 0.30 OR lower 95% CI < 0.18 on all sufficiently-powered arms.\n")
        lines.append("This implies ΔR²_color ≥ 0.30 is unachievable by any decoder-free objective "
                     "tested to date. Per the pre-registration, the project accepts the current "
                     "representation quality per ARM 1 verdict.\n")
    lines.append("\n")

    analysis_path = os.path.join(results_dir, "arm2_analysis.md")
    with open(analysis_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("".join(lines))
    print(f"Saved analysis to {analysis_path}")


def main():
    cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 2
    default_workers = min(cpu_count - 1, 4)
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Iter_030 ARM 2: M2 Decisive Test")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=default_workers)
    parser.add_argument("--sequential", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default=None)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    dry_run = args.dry_run
    seeds = args.seeds if args.seeds is not None else SEEDS
    max_workers = args.workers
    if args.device is not None:
        device = torch.device(args.device)
    else:
        device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    device_str = str(device)
    print(f"Device: {device} | Dry-run: {dry_run} | Seeds: {seeds} | Workers: {max_workers if not args.sequential else 1}")

    results_dir = "archive/iter_030/results"
    runs_dir = os.path.join(results_dir, "arm2_runs")
    checkpoints_dir = os.path.join(results_dir, "arm2_checkpoints")
    os.makedirs(runs_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(checkpoints_dir, exist_ok=True)

    # Parameter counts
    print("\n=== Parameter Counts ===")
    for arm in ARMS:
        m = NonParametricJEPASpatialSeparateDyn(
            d_max=arm["d_max"], h=3, k=4, cooldown=300, stabilization_period=100,
            pos_encoding=arm["pos_encoding"], primary_objective=arm["primary_objective"],
            sfa_weight=arm.get("sfa_weight", 0.0), gdasr_log_only=True,
            dyn_readout="mean", sub_features=1, dyn_source="spatial",
            contrastive_weight=arm.get("contrastive_weight", 5.0),
            temperature=arm.get("temperature", 0.1),
            mask_dyn_sim=arm["mask_dyn_sim"], coord_vicreg=arm["coord_vicreg"])
        pc = count_parameters(m)
        print(f"  {arm['name']}: {pc} parameters")
    print()

    tasks = [(arm, seed, device_str, dry_run, runs_dir, checkpoints_dir) for arm in ARMS for seed in seeds]
    total_tasks = len(tasks)
    print(f"Total tasks: {total_tasks}")
    all_results = []
    if args.sequential:
        for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
            print(f"\n{'='*70}\nTASK [{i+1}/{total_tasks}]: {task[0]['name']} seed={task[1]}\n{'='*70}")
            res = _run_single_worker(task); all_results.append(res)
    else:
        with concurrent.futures.ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
            future_to_label = {executor.submit(_run_single_worker, task): f"{task[0]['name']} seed={task[1]}" for task in tasks}
            for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed(future_to_label):
                label = future_to_label[future]
                try:
                    res = future.result(); all_results.append(res)
                    print(f"COMPLETED: {label}")
                except Exception as exc:
                    print(f"FAILED: {label} -> {exc}")
    df_all = pd.DataFrame(all_results)

    # Save merged outputs
    per_seed_path = os.path.join(results_dir, "arm2_per_seed.csv")
    df_all.to_csv(per_seed_path, index=False)
    print(f"\nSaved per-seed results to {per_seed_path}")

    # Summary table
    summary_rows = []
    for arm_name in [a["name"] for a in ARMS]:
        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]
        if len(df_arm) == 0:
            continue
        df_nc = df_arm[~df_arm["collapsed"]]
        n_nc = len(df_nc)
        if n_nc >= 5:
            mean_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].mean()
            std_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].std(ddof=1)
            se_dr2 = std_dr2 / math.sqrt(n_nc)
            ci_lower = mean_dr2 - 1.96 * se_dr2
        elif n_nc > 0:
            mean_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].mean()
            std_dr2 = df_nc["delta_r2_color"].std(ddof=1)
            se_dr2 = std_dr2 / math.sqrt(n_nc) if n_nc > 0 else float('nan')
            ci_lower = float('nan')
        else:
            mean_dr2 = float('nan'); std_dr2 = float('nan'); se_dr2 = float('nan'); ci_lower = float('nan')
        summary_rows.append({
            "arm": arm_name,
            "n_total": len(df_arm),
            "n_collapsed": int(df_arm["collapsed"].sum()),
            "n_non_collapsed": n_nc,
            "mean_delta_r2_color": mean_dr2 if not math.isnan(mean_dr2) else None,
            "std_delta_r2_color": std_dr2 if not math.isnan(std_dr2) else None,
            "se_delta_r2_color": se_dr2 if not math.isnan(se_dr2) else None,
            "ci_lower_95": ci_lower if not math.isnan(ci_lower) else None,
            "mean_centroid_mse": df_arm["centroid_mse_mean"].mean(),
            "mean_vicreg_mean_abs_corr": df_arm["vicreg_mean_abs_corr"].mean(),
        })
    df_summary = pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)
    summary_path = os.path.join(results_dir, "arm2_summary.csv")
    df_summary.to_csv(summary_path, index=False)
    print(f"Saved summary to {summary_path}")

    _generate_analysis(df_all, results_dir)
    print("\nDone.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()]

**Dual collapse criterion:** collapsed = collapsed_eval OR collapsed_train
**Contrastive implementation:** Existing batch-level NT-Xent in model.forward (same batch index as positive pair, cross-batch as negatives). Object-level temporal matching NOT implemented — uses weaker batch-level signal.
**D2 ramp:** sfa_weight linearly 0 → 5.0 over steps 1–4000, then held at 5.0 for steps 4001–8000.
